<script type="text/javascript">
var supportedLanguages = [ { Code: 'en', Description: 'English' }, { Code: 'es', Description: 'Spanish' }, { Code: 'de', Description: 'German' }, { Code: 'fr', Description: 'French' }, { Code: 'it', Description: 'Italian' }, { Code: 'pt', Description: 'Portuguese' }, { Code: 'nl', Description: 'Dutch' } ];

<script type="text/javascript">
    function setLanguage(lang) {
      //Set language in Localize library.
      Localize.setLanguage(lang);

      //Set language name in dropdown.
      $('.langDropdown').find('.language-name').text(getLanguageFullName(lang));
      
      Greenvelope.UI.Modal('#translationLoadingPopup');
      location.reload();
    };

    function getLanguageFullName(code) {
      var rv = 'English';
      //supportedLanguages variable is writter on master page.
      $(supportedLanguages).each(function () {
        var lang = this;
        if (lang.Code === code) {
          rv = lang.Description;
        }
      });
      return rv;
    };

    $(function () {
      //Set initial language value.
      var lang = Localize.getLanguage();

      //Set language name in dropdown.
      $('.langDropdown').find('.language-name').text(getLanguageFullName(lang));

      //Set available languages.
      var dd = $('.langDropdown').find(".dropdown-menu");
      if (!isEmpty(dropdownLanguages)) {
        // Product landing page with different url per language
        $(dropdownLanguages).each(function () {
          var lang = this;
          if (lang.Code != 'en') {
            dd.append('<li><a href="/' + lang.Code + '/' + lang.URL + '">' + lang.Description + '</a></li>');
          } else {
            dd.append('<li><a href="' + lang.URL + '?ljs=en">' + lang.Description + '</a></li>');
          }
        });
      } else {
        // Non product landing page with same URL for each language
        //supportedLanguages variable is writter on master page.
        $(supportedLanguages).each(function () {
          var lang = this;
          if (lang.Code != 'en') {
            dd.append('<li><a href="/' + lang.Code + relativeURL + '">' + lang.Description + '</a></li>');
          } else {
            dd.append('<li><a href="' + relativeURL + '?ljs=en">' + lang.Description + '</a></li>');
          }
        });
      }

      // Hide Zendesk chat for non-english viewers
      if (lang != 'en') {
        zE('webWidget', 'hide');
      }      
    });
  </script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    var imagesLoaded = 0;

    $("document").ready(function () {

      ko.applyBindings(new LandingViewModel(), document.getElementById("page"));      
  	  updateLayout();

  	  $('.navbar li a, .scroll a').click(function (event) {
  	    var scrollPos = $('body').find($(this).attr('href')).offset().top + 0;
  	    var hash = this.hash;
  	    $('html,body').animate({
  	      scrollTop: scrollPos
  	    }, 500, function () {
  	      window.location.hash = hash;
  	    });
  	    return false;
  	  });

  	  $("#slideshow").owlCarousel({
  	    nav: false, // Show next and prev buttons
  	    dots: $("#slideshow > .item").length > 1,
        autoplay: true,
        lazyLoad: true,
        autoplayTimeout: 6000,
  	    slideSpeed: 300,
  	    paginationSpeed: 400,
  	    items: 1
  	  });

  	  //$("#slideshow-testimony").owlCarousel({

     //   autoplay: true,

  	    pageLayout();
  	  });

  	  Fluidvids.init({
  	    selector: '#videoSample',
        players: ['player.vimeo.com']
      });
    });

    function pageLayout() {
      if ($(window).height() > 770) {
        // Background height
        backgroundHeight = $(window).height() - 40;


        // Fix for Safari iFrame issue
        if (window.top !== window.self) {
          if (backgroundHeight > window.screen.availHeight && window.screen.availHeight > 0) {
            $('.landing').css('max-width', window.screen.availWidth + 20);
            backgroundHeight = window.screen.availHeight;
          }
        }

        $('#divBG').css('height', backgroundHeight);

        // Top Margin (for vertical centering)
        var topMargin = ((backgroundHeight - 500) / 2.5) + 70;
        $('.heading .left').css('margin-top', topMargin + 'px');
        $('.heading .right').css('margin-top', (topMargin - 140) + 'px');
      } else {
        $('.heading .left').css('margin-top', '');
        $('.heading .right').css('margin-top', '');
      }

      navbar();
      checkIfVideoInView();
    }

    function navbar() {
      if ($(window).scrollTop() >= backgroundHeight) {
        $('#navbar').addClass('navbar-fixed-top');
        $('body').css('margin-top', '51px');
      } else {
        $('#navbar').removeClass('navbar-fixed-top');
        $('body').css('margin-top', '0px');
      }
    }

    function checkIfVideoInView() {
      if (videoPlayed == false && $('#videoSample')[0] != null) {
        if (isScrolledIntoViewMiddle($('#videoSample')[0])) {
          if (!isIE8()) { $f($('#videoSample')[0]).api('play'); }
          videoPlayed = true;
        }
      }
    }

    function updateLayout() {
      var owl = $("#designList").data('owlCarousel');
      if (owl != null) { owl.destroy(); }

      var designListPgCount = Number($("#designListCount").val()) / 4;
      
      $("#designList").owlCarousel({
        nav: designListPgCount > 1, // Show next and prev buttons
        dots: designListPgCount > 1,
        lazyLoad: true,
        autoplay: true,
        autoplayTimeout: 6000,
        slideSpeed: 750,
        paginationSpeed: 750,
        scrollPerPage: true,
        loop: true,
        responsive: {
          0: {
            items: 1
          },
          550: {
            items: 2
          },
          1160: {
            items: 4
          },
        }
      });

      bindTooltips();
      $("#designList").on('changed.owl.carousel', bindTooltips)    
    }

<div class="col-md-4 text-center">

<div class="padding-b-20">
<span class="font-size-24">Delivery Optimizer</span>

</div>
</div>
<div class="col-md-4 text-center">
<img alt="auto reminders" class="image-sending" data-bind="attr: { src: ListMgtTab() == 'Sending' ? 'https://cdnpng.greenvelope.com/pub/_images/landing/v3/ic-auto-reminders.png' : 'about:blank' }" src="about:blank" style="height: 50px; margin-top: 60px;"/><br/><br/><br/>
<div class="padding-b-20">

<!-- Testimonials -->
<!-- Making a Difference -->
<div class="text-center padding-t-60 making-difference" style="background-color: #FFFFFF; min-height: 350px;">
<div class="container">
<h2 class="margin-b-40">Making A Difference</h2>
<div class="font-size-16" style="max-width: 970px; line-height: 30px; margin: 0 auto; margin-bottom: 30px;">
<span isolate="">By creating a positive online experience, we hope more hosts will consider sending invitations electronically for formal events – to help save trees, time, and money. To express our commitment towards this goal, we donate a percentage of every sale to Mountains to Sound, a non-profit organization that maintains forests. Together we can "invite" a greener future.</span><br/><br/>
<br/><a class="btnV2 btnV2-white" href="about" isolate="">Learn More about Greenvelope</a><br/><br/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
